enum devlink\+\_\+port\+\_\+fn\+\_\+opstate -\/ indicates operational state of the function @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+P\+O\+R\+T\+\_\+\+F\+N\+\_\+\+O\+P\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+A\+T\+T\+A\+C\+H\+ED\+: Driver is attached to the function. For graceful tear down of the function, after inactivation of the function, user should wait for operational state to turn D\+E\+T\+A\+C\+H\+ED. @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+P\+O\+R\+T\+\_\+\+F\+N\+\_\+\+O\+P\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+D\+E\+T\+A\+C\+H\+ED\+: Driver is detached from the function. It is safe to delete the port. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{devlink\_trap\_action}{devlink\_trap\_action}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2devlink_8h_a03e57f52dafc5c025848ea010936aeaa}{devlink\+\_\+trap\+\_\+action}}}

enum devlink\+\_\+trap\+\_\+action -\/ \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolPacket-p}{Packet}} trap action. @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+C\+T\+I\+O\+N\+\_\+\+D\+R\+OP\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolPacket-p}{Packet}} is dropped by the device and a copy is not sent to the C\+PU. @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+C\+T\+I\+O\+N\+\_\+\+T\+R\+AP\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} sole copy of the packet is sent to the C\+PU. @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+P\+\_\+\+A\+C\+T\+I\+O\+N\+\_\+\+M\+I\+R\+R\+OR\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolPacket-p}{Packet}} is forwarded by the device and a copy is sent to the C\+PU. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}

\index{devlink\_trap\_type@{devlink\_trap\_type}!devlink.h@{devlink.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{devlink\_trap\_type}{devlink\_trap\_type}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2devlink_8h_a444e823503dd2ebc366c252ce687402d}{devlink\+\_\+trap\+\_\+type}}}

enum devlink\+\_\+trap\+\_\+type -\/ \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolPacket-p}{Packet}} trap type. @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+D\+R\+OP\+: Trap reason is a drop. Trapped packets are only processed by devlink and not injected to the kernel\textquotesingle{}s Rx path. @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+E\+X\+C\+E\+P\+T\+I\+ON\+: Trap reason is an exception. \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolPacket-p}{Packet}} was not forwarded as intended due to an exception (e.\+g., missing neighbour entry) and trapped to control plane for resolution. Trapped packets are processed by devlink and injected to the kernel\textquotesingle{}s Rx path. @\+D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+R\+OL\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolPacket-p}{Packet}} was trapped because it is required for the correct functioning of the control plane. For example, an A\+RP request packet. Trapped packets are injected to the kernel\textquotesingle{}s Rx path, but not reported to drop monitor. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}
\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{DEVLINK\_TRAP\_TYPE\_DROP@{DEVLINK\_TRAP\_TYPE\_DROP}!devlink.h@{devlink.h}}\index{devlink.h@{devlink.h}!DEVLINK\_TRAP\_TYPE\_DROP@{DEVLINK\_TRAP\_TYPE\_DROP}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2devlink_8h_a444e823503dd2ebc366c252ce687402da6e71b8f715bcf8aa7f4e667fb1e67a7d}\label{uapi_2linux_2devlink_8h_a444e823503dd2ebc366c252ce687402da6e71b8f715bcf8aa7f4e667fb1e67a7d}} 
D\+E\+V\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+D\+R\+OP&\\
\hline
